Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The enumerative operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 138. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 969 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on enumerative structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 138. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 969. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the enumerative object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 75. The moduli space has dimension at most 557 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 75 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the enumerative structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 557. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
